# H1 — Intimacy (functional re-coding)

Run: `v4_l12_granular_final_call49` — 98 topics.

### New categories (read these before the topics)

| Code | Category | Definition |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `I0` | **off target** | Not about romantic intimacy as defined below. Includes domestic errands, meals, animals, logistics, medical care, mentoring, generic plot talk, and non-romantic help. Prefer I0 over stretching any intimacy code. |
| `I1` | **emotional intimacy** | Disclosure, vulnerability, shared secrets, emotional closeness WITHOUT physical contact and WITHOUT reassurance-as-comfort as the main act. |
| `I2` | **reassurance** | Comforting, soothing, affirming safety/worth/the bond ("you'll be fine", "I won't hurt you", fever checks, watch-while-sleeping caretalk). Not kissing/holding. |
| `I3` | **nonexplicit affection** | ONLY when the dominant content is affectionate physical contact that is NOT genital/oral/penetrative: kissing, hugging, holding, caressing, undressing-as-prelude, non-genital touch. Ban I3 if there is no such contact in keywords/sentences. Kissing/clothes-removal alone are I3, never I6. |
| `I4` | **sexual tension** | Desire, anticipation, charged looking/invitation WITHOUT consummation and WITHOUT affectionate contact as the main act (eyes meeting, lip biting, longing, "want you"). |
| `I5` | **erotic escalation** | Building toward sex; heated undressing/touch progressing toward an explicit act, but not yet full explicit-sex focus. |
| `I6` | **explicit sex** | Explicit sexual acts (genital contact, oral sex, penetration, orgasm-focused intercourse). Do NOT code kissing, undressing, or non-genital caressing as I6 — those are I3. |
| `I7` | **aftercare** | Post-sex tenderness, checking in, bonding immediately after intimacy. |
| `I8` | **negotiation** | Consent talk, boundaries, stop-when-asked, preferences about intimacy/sex. |
| `I9` | **coercive sexuality** | Pressure, force, non-consent, or ambiguous consent around sex. |
| `I10` | **eroticized gaze** | Objectifying / erotic looking or appearance-focused desire without mutual intimacy or affectionate contact (watching body, beauty compliments as erotic appraisal). |

The taxonomy confirms this cluster centers on physical vulnerability and caretaking/reassurance — neither of which requires affectionate physical contact to function narratively. No kissing, hugging, holding, caressing, or undressing-as-prelude is evidenced. The secondary construct (4.6 Emotional Safety, Reassurance & Caretaking) is consistent with I2 adjacency, but without contact evidence the anti-collapse rules prohibit elevation to I3. I0 is the correct and most specific code for domestic/care/HEA-adjacent content without physical intimacy contact. No split or reinterpretation is warranted.
